% The macro names the manuscript used when N11 Stage 2 (code/scripts/n11_stage2.py analyse2)
% and N11 Stage 3 (code/scripts/n11_forecast.py analyse) ran. Both scripts write a
% decision-time twin of a published macro only if the manuscript uses its name, and
% n11_stage2.manuscript_tokens() reads the names from every .tex file in this folder
% together with main.tex and supplement.tex. This release holds no manuscript text, so
% this file stands in for it. It lists, one per line, each name whose twin the two runs
% wrote, read back from analysis/N11_macros.tex and analysis/N11F_macros.tex; names that
% match no published macro select nothing.
% 8594 names, listed when this release was built.
